Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on dp structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 449. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 55 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 449 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the dp structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 55. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The dp operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 683. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 286 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to dp theory, we decompose the problem into 683 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 286. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
